class="hero hero--default" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp0" identifier="lead-contentpage-with-checkbox"><div class="container container--grid gap--responsive"><div class="hero__content"><p class="meta-info"><!--[--><!--]--></p><h1 class="h1 hero__title">Information on GovCERT</h1><!----><p class="font--regular hero__description">The Government Computer Emergency Response Team (GovCERT) is the national specialist service responsible for the technical management of cyberincidents and technical analysis of cyberthreats. It supports critical infrastructure operators, the public sector and the Swiss business location with technical information on current cyberthreats and with the management of cyberincidents. GovCERT also works in close collaboration with the police authorities. This cooperation covers both the exchange of information and support with technical analyses.</p><!----><!----><!----></div><div class="hero__image"><div class=""><figure><picture><!--[--><source aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" srcset="https://prod-bacsch-hcms-sdweb.imgix.net/dam/en/sd-web/BHVbOBH7sDBg/foto-govcert-v2-blau-s.png?auto=format&amp;w=4096 4096w, https://prod-bacsch-hcms-sdweb.imgix.net/dam/en/sd-web/BHVbOBH7sDBg/foto-govcert-v2-blau-s.png?auto=format&amp;w=2048 2048w, https://prod-bacsch-hcms-sdweb.imgix.net/dam/en/sd-web/BHVbOBH7sDBg/foto-govcert-v2-blau-s.png?auto=format&amp;w=1240 1240w, https://prod-bacsch-hcms-sdweb.imgix.net/dam/en/sd-web/BHVbOBH7sDBg/foto-govcert-v2-blau-s.png?auto=format&amp;w=640 640w" media="(min-resolution: 2dppx)"><source aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" tabindex="-1" src="https://prod-bacsch-hcms-sdweb.imgix.net/dam/en/sd-web/BHVbOBH7sDBg/foto-govcert-v2-blau-s.png?w=1024&amp;auto=format"><!----></picture><!----></figure><div></div></div><!----><!----></div></div></section><!--]--><!--[--><section class="section section--default" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp4" identifier="section"><!--[--><!--[--><div id="doc-container-grid-1787625740571-774" class="container container--grid gap--responsive" identifier="container-grid"><div class="container__center--xs vertical-spacing"><!--[--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp6" identifier="p">To ensure the timely exchange of information on cyberthreats, GovCERT works with other CERTs and CSIRTs (computer security incident response teams) worldwide. In addition, GovCERT is a member of various recognised international organisations.</p><!--]--><!--[--><h2 id="Blog-and-white-papers" class="h2" identifier="heading-h2" anchornav="Blog-and-white-papers">Blog and white papers</h2><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp8" identifier="p">Over the years, GovCERT has published a variety of blogs, white papers (technical reports) and other documentation on cyberthreats (previously on www.govcert.ch).<br />
 These documents will now be published on GitHub:</p><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp9" identifier="p"><a href="https://github.com/govcert-ch/website/" target="_blank" rel="external" class="link--external" aria-label="GitHub: Archive of the GovCERT.ch website Open link in new tab">GitHub: Archive of the GovCERT.ch website</a></p><!--]--><!--[--><h2 id="List-of-blocked-file-types" class="h2" identifier="heading-h2" anchornav="List-of-blocked-file-types">List of blocked file types</h2><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp11" identifier="p">GovCERT publishes a list of file types that are often used to distribute malware by email, and recommends that these be proactively blocked at the email gateway or spam filter, or placed in quarantine.</p><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp12" identifier="p"><a href="https://github.com/govcert-ch/misc/blob/main/blocked-filetypes/extensions.txt" target="_blank" rel="external" class="link--external" aria-label="GitHub: List of blocked file types Open link in new tab">GitHub: List of blocked file types</a></p><!--]--><!--[--><h2 id="International-cooperation" class="h2" identifier="heading-h2" anchornav="International-cooperation">International cooperation</h2><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp14" identifier="p">In the joint fight against cyberthreats, international cooperation is very important. Membership in various associations enables GovCERT to establish a global network of personal relationships. GovCERT is a member of FIRST (Forum of Incident Response and Security Teams, www.first.org), of the European Government CERTs (EGC) group (www.egc-group.org) and of the International Watch and Warning Network (IWWN). In addition, GovCERT has accreditation from the European Trusted Introducer (TI) Service.</p><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp15" identifier="p"><a href="https://www.first.org/members/teams/ncsc-ch" target="_blank" rel="external" class="link--external" aria-label="Forum of Incident Response and Security Teams FIRST Open link in new tab">Forum of Incident Response and Security Teams FIRST</a></p><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp16" identifier="p"><a href="https://www.egc-group.org/" target="_blank" rel="external" class="link--external" aria-label="European Government CERTs EGC Open link in new tab">European Government CERTs EGC</a></p><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp17" identifier="p"><a href="https://www.trusted-introducer.org/trusted-introducer/directory/teams/govcertch/" target="_blank" rel="external" class="link--external" aria-label="Trusted Introducer Open link in new tab">Trusted Introducer</a></p><!--]--><!--[--><h2 id="Contacts" class="h2" identifier="heading-h2" anchornav="Contacts">Contacts</h2><!--]--><!--[--><h3 id="doc-1jpk8ifpp19" class="h3" identifier="heading-h3" anchornav="Reports-on-cyberincidents-from-critical-infrastructures">Reports on cyberincidents from critical infrastructures</h3><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp20" identifier="p">Reports on cyberincidents from critical infrastructures or cybersecurity specialists can be sent to GovCERT at the following email address:</p><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp21" identifier="p">incidents[at]govcert{dot}ch</p><!--]--><!--[--><h3 id="doc-1jpk8ifpp22" class="h3" identifier="heading-h3" anchornav="Technical-queries">Technical queries</h3><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp23" identifier="p">Other technical queries that do not concern a specific cyberincident can be sent to GovCERT at the following email address:</p><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp24" identifier="p">outreach[at]govcert{dot}ch</p><!--]--><!--[--><h3 id="doc-1jpk8ifpp25" class="h3" identifier="heading-h3" anchornav="Encryption-Keys">Encryption Keys</h3><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp26" identifier="p">These two GovCERT mailboxes support PGP and S/MIME, which allow secure and phishing-resistant email communication. The<br />
 corresponding keys can be found here:<br /></p><!--]--><!--[--><div class="container__aside" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp27" identifier="manual-download-list" linktext linktargetlabelblank="Open link in new reports on cyberincidents</h3><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp33" identifier="p">General reports on cyberincidents can be sent to the NCSC using the web form. This helps the NCSC to identify potential online threat trends and take targeted action to combat them:</p><!--]--><!--[--><p class="font--regular" id="doc-1jpk8ifpp34" identifier="p"><a href="https://www.report.ncsc.admin.ch/en/" target="_blank" rel="external" class="link--external" aria-label="NCSC reporting form Open link in new tab">NCSC reporting form</a></p><!--]--><!--]--></div><!----></div><!--]--><!--]--></section><!--]--><!--]--></div><!----></div><!--]--><div class="back-to-top-wrapper"><a href="#main-header" role="button" type="button" aria-label="Scroll to top" class="back-to-top-btn back-to-top-btn--fixed back-to-top-btn--outline"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" class="icon icon--base back-to-top-btn__icon"><path d="m19.044 14.731-6.669-3.85-6.669 3.85-.375-.649 7.044-4.067 7.044 4.067z"></path></svg></a></div></main><span></span><footer id="main-footer" class="bg--secondary-600"><div><!--[--><div class="bg--secondary-600"><div class="container"><div class="footer-information"><div class="footer-information__entry"><h2 class="footer-information__entry-title">About us</h2><p>The National Cyber Security Centre is the federal government's competence centre for cybersecurity and thus the first point of contact for businesses, public services, educational institutions and the population when it comes to cyberissues. It is responsible for the coordinated implementation of the national cyberstrategy.</p><!----></div><div class="footer-information__entry"><h2 class="footer-information__entry-title">Stay
